Sub PrintForm()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	satirBasla = 4

	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	logo = trim(Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm) & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	if not fso.FileExists(logo) Then logo = ""

	MMPS10T_LIST.Sort("REVURETIMDENTESTARIH")

	Sh1.Cells(1, 1).Select
	if trim(logo) <> "" Then
		Sh1.Pictures.Insert(logo).Select
	End if

	Sutun = 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "MPS No"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Sipariþ No"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Müþteri Sipariþ No"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Firma"
'	Sutun = Sutun + 1
'	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Stok Kodu"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Stok Adý"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Ýþ Emri Miktarý"
	IEMikCol = Sutun
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Termin"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Kalan Gün"
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "MMPS10T_LIST")
	for i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		if Left(Grid(i,0), 5) = "COLVT" and Grid(i,2) <> "..." Then
			Sutun = Sutun + 1
			Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = trim(Grid(i,2))
		End if
	Next
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Sevkiyat Miktarý"
	SevkCol = Sutun
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Rezerve Miktar"
	RezCol = Sutun
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, 1), Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

	satirNo = satirBasla
	QStr = "SELECT S01.KOD, STOK40T.EVRAKNO SIPNO, STOK40T.CHSIPNO CHSIPNO, MMPS10TM.EVRAKNO MPSNO, STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU,"
	QStr = QStr & " STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR SIPMIKTAR, STOK40T.REV_RTESTARIH,(STOK40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - STOK40T.SF_SEVKDEMIK) SEVKMIK,"
	QStr = QStr & " S01.SF_MIKTAR REZ_MIKTAR, MMPS10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR ISEMRIMIK"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM (SELECT KOD, ALLOC_SIPARTNO, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR "
	QStr = QStr & " 	FROM STOK01 "
	QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE STOK01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO<>'                '"
	QStr = QStr & " 	GROUP BY KOD, ALLOC_SIPARTNO) S01"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T ON STOK40T.ARTNO=S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10TM ON STOK40T.ARTNO=MMPS10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE MMPS10TM.ACIK_KAPALI='K'"

	Doc.RunSQLQuery "XSDSS", QStr
	Set S01X = Doc.GetTableObject("XSDSS")
	For i = 1 to S01X.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Kapalý MPS 'lere Ait Bilgiler Excel 'e aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S01X.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		Sutun = 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = S01X.MPSNO(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Select
		Excel.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 36
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(S01X.SIPNO(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(S01X.CHSIPNO(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S01X.MUSTERIKODU(i), ""))
'		Sutun = Sutun + 1
'		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(MMPS10T_LIST.R_MAMULKODU(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01X.KOD(i), ""))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		IsEmriMiktari = S01X.ISEMRIMIK(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = IsEmriMiktari / 1000
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		TeslimTarihi = S01X.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		TeslimTarihi = DateSerial(Doc.StrDate_Year(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Month(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Day(TeslimTarihi))
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = TeslimTarihi
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Doc.CDateToInt(TeslimTarihi) - Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.BuGun)

		SevkMik = S01X.SEVKMIK(i) / 1000
		if SevkMik > 0 Then Sh1.Cells(satirNo, SevkCol).Value = SevkMik
		RezerveMiktar = S01X.REZ_MIKTAR(i) / 1000
		if RezerveMiktar > 0 Then Sh1.Cells(satirNo, RezCol).Value = RezerveMiktar
	Next
	For i = 1 to MMPS10T_LIST.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & MMPS10T_LIST.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		Sutun = 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 1).Value = MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i)
		SipArr = GetSipArr(MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & SipArr(0)
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & SipArr(1)
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & SipArr(2)
'		Sutun = Sutun + 1
'		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(MMPS10T_LIST.R_MAMULKODU(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = trim(MMPS10T_LIST.R_MAMULKODU_STOK00_AD(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		IsEmriMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i), "")
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = IsEmriMiktari / 1000
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		TeslimTarihi = MMPS10T_LIST.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i)
		TeslimTarihi = DateSerial(Doc.StrDate_Year(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Month(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Day(TeslimTarihi))
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = TeslimTarihi
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Doc.CDateToInt(MMPS10T_LIST.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i)) - Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.BuGun)
		Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "MMPS10T_LIST")
		DegerColBasla = Sutun + 1
		for j = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
			if Left(Grid(j,0), 5) = "COLVT" and Grid(j,2) <> "..." Then
				Sutun = Sutun + 1
				Deger = MMPS10T_LIST.GetFieldValue(Grid(j,0), i)
				DegerArr = FormatDeger(Deger)
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = DegerArr(0)
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Select
				if DegerArr(1) <> 0 Then
					Excel.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = DegerArr(1)
				End if
			End if
		Next
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		if SipArr(4) > 0 Then Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = SipArr(4) / 1000
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		if SipArr(3) > 0 Then Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = SipArr(3) / 1000
	Next
	sagSutun = Sutun

	QStr = "SELECT STOK40T.EVRAKNO, STOK40T.KOD, STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU, STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR, STOK40T.REV_RTESTARIH, STOK40T.CHSIPNO, STOK40T.ARTNO FROM STOK40T"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10TM ON STOK40T.ARTNO=MMPS10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK40T.ACIKKAPALI=' ' AND MMPS10TM.EVRAKNO IS NULL ORDER BY REV_RTESTARIH"
	Doc.RunSQLQuery "weg4tgt5", QStr
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("weg4tgt5")
	For i = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'e Baðlý Olmayan Sipariþler Excel 'e aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S40T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		Sutun = 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = ""
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(S40T.EVRAKNO(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(S40T.CHSIPNO(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S40T.MUSTERIKODU(i), ""))
'		Sutun = Sutun + 1
'		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(MMPS10T_LIST.R_MAMULKODU(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S40T.KOD(i), ""))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		IsEmriMiktari = S40T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = IsEmriMiktari / 1000
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		TeslimTarihi = S40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		TeslimTarihi = DateSerial(Doc.StrDate_Year(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Month(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Day(TeslimTarihi))
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = TeslimTarihi
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Doc.CDateToInt(TeslimTarihi) - Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.BuGun)

		RezerveMiktar = GetSipeRezerveMik(S40T.ARTNO(i))
		if RezerveMiktar > 0 Then Sh1.Cells(satirNo, RezCol).Value = RezerveMiktar
	Next

	Call NumaraFormatla(Excel, Sh1, IEMikCol, satirBasla + 1, satirNo)
	Call NumaraFormatla(Excel, Sh1, SevkCol, satirBasla + 1, satirNo)
	Call NumaraFormatla(Excel, Sh1, RezCol, satirBasla + 1, satirNo)


	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(satirBasla,1),Sh1.Cells(satirNo,sagSutun)).Select 
	Excel.Selection.Font.Name = "Arial Narrow"

	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(satirBasla+1, DegerColBasla), Sh1.Cells(satirNo, sagSutun)).Select
	Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = -4152

	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(satirBasla,1),Sh1.Cells(satirBasla,sagSutun)).Select 
	Sh1.Rows(satirBasla).RowHeight = 26.25
	Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = -4160
	Excel.Selection.WrapText = True
	
	Excel.Selection.EntireColumn.AutoFit
	Excel.Columns(4).ColumnWidth = 11.71

	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.PrintTitleRows = "$1:$" & satirBasla

	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.PrintArea = Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(1,1),Sh1.Cells(satirNo,sagSutun)).Address
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.LeftMargin = 28.3 * 0.8
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.RightMargin = 28.3 * 0.8
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.TopMargin = 28.3 * 1
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.BottomMargin = 28.3 * 1
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.FooterMargin = 28.3 * 0.8
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.CenterHorizontally = True
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.Orientation = 2 'landscape
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.PaperSize = 9 'A4
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.FitToPagesWide = 1
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.FitToPagesTall = False
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.Zoom = False
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.CenterFooter = "&P/&N"

	Sh1.Cells(2,1).Value = "ÜRETÝM GAZETESÝ"
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(2,1),Sh1.Cells(2,sagSutun)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Size = 18
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = -4108
	Excel.Selection.Merge

	Sh1.Cells(1, sagSutun - 3).Value = "Tarih"
	Sh1.Cells(1, sagSutun - 3).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Sh1.Cells(1, sagSutun - 2).Value = ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun) & " " & Doc.Saat(5)


	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(satirBasla,1),Sh1.Cells(satirNo,sagSutun)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Borders(7).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(7).Weight = 2
	Excel.Selection.Borders(8).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(8).Weight = 2
	Excel.Selection.Borders(9).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(9).Weight = 2
	Excel.Selection.Borders(10).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(10).Weight = 2
	Excel.Selection.Borders(11).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(11).Weight = 2
	Excel.Selection.Borders(12).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(12).Weight = 2
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, 1).Select
	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub
Function GetSipArr(MPSNo)
	Dim SipArr(4)
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dwrw3gtt43", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'", "", -1)
	Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select2("d35355", M10TM.GetAppName, "MUSTERISIPINO<>'          '", "", -1)

	M10TM1.GroupBy("MUSTERISIPINO")
	For i = 1 to M10TM1.GetRowCount()
		SipArr(0) = SipArr(0) & ", " & trim(M10TM1.MUSTERISIPINO(i))
		CHSipNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", M10TM1.MUSTERISIPINO(i), "")
		if trim(CHSipNo) <> "" Then
			SipArr(1) = SipArr(1) & ", " & trim(CHSipNo)		
		End if
	Next
	if trim(SipArr(0)) <> "" Then SipArr(0) = Mid(SipArr(0), 3)
	if trim(SipArr(1)) <> "" Then SipArr(1) = Mid(SipArr(1), 3)

	Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select2("d35355", M10TM.GetAppName, "MUSTERIKODU<>'          '", "", -1)
	M10TM1.GroupBy("MUSTERIKODU")
	For i = 1 to M10TM1.GetRowCount()
		SipArr(2) = SipArr(2) & ", " & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", M10TM1.MUSTERIKODU(i), ""))
	Next 
	if trim(SipArr(2)) <> "" Then SipArr(2) = Mid(SipArr(2), 3)
	
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		if trim(M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i)) <> "" Then
			SipArr(3) = SipArr(3) + GetSipeRezerveMik(M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i))
			Set S40T = Doc.Select1("fsw46yy", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			SipArr(4) = SipArr(4) + S40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - S40T.SF_SEVKDEMIK
		End if
	Next
	GetSipArr = SipArr
End Function
Function FormatDeger(Deger)
	Dim DegerArray(1)
	Deger = Replace(Deger, "[", "")
	Deger = Replace(Deger, "]", "")
	DegerArr = Split(Deger, "-")
	if uBound(DegerArr) = 2 Then
		DegerArr(0) = Replace(DegerArr(0), ",", "")
		DegerArr(1) = Replace(DegerArr(1), ",", "")
		YMMiktar = Doc.CDbl(DegerArr(0))
		TamamlananMiktar = Doc.CDbl(DegerArr(1))
		if TamamlananMiktar >= YMMiktar Then
			DegerArray(1) = 36
		Elseif TamamlananMiktar > 0 Then
			DegerArray(1) = 35
		Else
			DegerArray(1) = 0
		End if

		DegerArr(0) = Doc.NumFormat0(YMMiktar / 1000, 12, 1)
		DegerArr(1) = Doc.NumFormat0(TamamlananMiktar / 1000, 12, 1)

		DegerArr(0) = TurkceFormat(DegerArr(0))
		DegerArr(1) = TurkceFormat(DegerArr(1))

		DegerArray(0) = DegerArr(1) & "/" & DegerArr(0)
	End if
	FormatDeger = DegerArray
End Function
Sub NumaraFormatla(Excel, Sh1, Sutun, BaslangicSatir, BitisSatir)
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(BaslangicSatir, Sutun), Sh1.Cells(BitisSatir, Sutun)).Select
	Excel.Selection.NumberFormat = "#,##0.0"
End Sub
Function TurkceFormat(SayiStr)
	SayiStr = Replace(SayiStr, ",", ";")
	SayiStr = Replace(SayiStr, ".", ",")
	SayiStr = Replace(SayiStr, ";", ".")
	TurkceFormat = SayiStr
End Function
Function GetSipeRezerveMik(SipArtNo)
	GetSipeRezerveMik = 0
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("we356tg3w4gw", "STOK01", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO='" & SipArtNo & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	S01.GroupBy("KOD")
	GetSipeRezerveMik = S01.SF_MIKTAR
End Function

Sub PrintForm()
	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Set S60E = Doc.Select1("nfdsjhfs","MMPS10E","EVRAKNO = '"& MMPS10E.EVRAKNO &"'","",-1)
	if S60E.GetRecCount() < 1 then
		Exit Sub
		msgbox "Evrak Kaydedilemedi!..."
	End if

	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297

	ustmargin = 10
	solmargin = 8

	Gen1 = 50
	Gen2 = 40
	Gen3 = 36

	satiryuksekligi = 6

	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Ýþ Emri", SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")

	res = pe1.nopen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin+1, solMargin+1, 18, Gen2+3, logo, 96) 

	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 24)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+4,SolMargin+5+Gen2, 16,275-5-Gen2,"Ý Þ   E M R Ý   T A K Ý P   F O R M U")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.box(UstMargin,SolMargin, 183, 281)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+20,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin,SolMargin+Gen2+5, 20, 0)

	Call Baslik()
	Call Satirlar()
	Call OperasyonMalzeme()

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 6)
	res = pe1.Write(sayfaBoyu-15,SolMargin, 6,60,"FRM.010/YT.03.10.2011/REV.0/1-REV.TRH.06.12.2013")
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(sayfaBoyu-16,sayfaeni-48,7,7)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(sayfaBoyu-16,sayfaeni-37, 12,30,"ÜRETÝM TALÝMATI MEVCUT KONTROLÜ")

	res = pe1.Show()
	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX" Then Doc.Save_Voucher

End Sub


Sub Baslik()
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)

	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom+5,SolMargin, 6,Gen1*2,"SÝPARÝÞ VERÝLERÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop-4,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,"SÝPARÝÞ MÝKTARI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,"PARTÝ MÝKTARI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,"SIRA NO")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,"SÝPARÝÞ TÜRÜ")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,"SÝPARÝÞ NO")

	Set M10TM = Doc.Select2("sfghe3dsds","MMPS10TM","","",1)
	
	Artno = M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(1)

	Set S40T = Doc.Select1("sf213ds","STOK40T","ARTNO = '"& Artno &"'","",1)
	if S40T.GetRecCount>0 then
		sipmik = S40T.SF_MIKTAR(1)
		sirano = ""
		siptur = ""
		sipno = M10TM.MUSTERISIPINO(1)
	End if
	parmik = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR

	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+7,SolMargin+Gen1*2, 6,Gen3,Doc.NumFormat(sipmik,10,0))
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,parmik)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,sirano)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,siptur)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,sipno)
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop-1-7,SolMargin+Gen1*2, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)

	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+6,SolMargin+Gen1*2, 0, 281-Gen1*2)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+9,SolMargin, 0, 281)

	res = pe1.Write(46,SolMargin, 6,Gen1,"ÜRÜN ADI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1,"GRUP ADI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1*2,"PARÇA ADI")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8.5)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2,"SÝPARÝÞÝN VER. TARÝH")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2,"MUHTEMEL SÝP. TES. TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)

	stokkodu = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU

	urun = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",stokkodu,"")
	grup = ""
	parca = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",stokkodu,"")
	siptar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","TARIH",sipno ,"")
	testar = MMPS10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH

	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+7,SolMargin, 6,Gen1,urun)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1,grup)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1*2,parca)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2,Doc.StrToNorDate(siptar))
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2,Doc.StrToNorDate(testar))

	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop-1-7,SolMargin+Gen1, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen1, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen1*2, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen2, 15, 0)

	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+6,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+9,SolMargin, 0, 281)


	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom+1,SolMargin, 6,Gen1*2,"PARÇA KODU")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1*2,"RESÝM NO")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2*2,"ÝÞ EMRÝ NO")

	pkodu = stokkodu
	resimno = stokkodu
	isemrino = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO

	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+7,SolMargin, 6,Gen1*2,pkodu)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1*2,resimno)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2*2,isemrino)

	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop-1-7,SolMargin+Gen1*2, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen1*2, 15, 0)

	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+6,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+9,SolMargin, 0, 281)

End Sub

Sub Satirlar()

Gen4 = 10
Gen5 = 90
Gen6 = 55
Gen7 = 75
Gen8 = 40

	res = pe1.Write(75+3,SolMargin, 10,Gen4+Gen5,"OPERASYON SIRALARI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 10,Gen4+Gen6+Gen7+Gen8,"ÜRÜN LÝSTESÝ")	

	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetBottom-3,SolMargin, 0, 281)

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,SolMargin+Gen4, satiryuksekligi*18, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop-10,Pe1.GetRight+Gen5, satiryuksekligi*18+10, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+10+satiryuksekligi,Pe1.GetRight+Gen4, satiryuksekligi*17, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen6, satiryuksekligi*17, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen7, satiryuksekligi*17, 0)


	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop-satiryuksekligi*2,SolMargin, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,i)

	for i = 1 to 17
'		res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetBottom+satiryuksekligi,SolMargin+Gen4+Gen5, 0, 281-(SolMargin+Gen4+Gen5))
		res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetBottom+satiryuksekligi,SolMargin+Gen4+Gen5, 0, 281-(Gen1*2))
	Next
'	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")

		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom-(satiryuksekligi*18),SolMargin, satiryuksekligi,Gen1*2,ii)
	for i = 1 to 9
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom+satiryuksekligi,SolMargin, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,i)
		res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetBottom,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	Next

	res = pe1.Write(75+10+1,SolMargin+Gen4+Gen5,satiryuksekligi,Gen4+Gen6+Gen7+Gen8,"ALT MALZEME GRUBU")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom,Pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,"S.NO")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi,Gen6,"KODU")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi,Gen7,"ADI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi,Gen8,"ADET(MIKTAR)")

	for i = 1 to 16
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom,SolMargin+Gen4+Gen5, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,i)
	Next

End Sub

Sub OperasyonMalzeme()

	Set M10T = Doc.Select2("sdsds","MMPS10T","R_KAYNAKTYPE = 'I'","",-1)

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	OperasyonMalzemeBalangic = 75+10+1

	for ii = 1 to M10T.GetRecCount()
		Sayi = Doc.Fmod(ii,2) = 1
		if Sayi <> "Doðru" then
			opr_sol = SolMargin+Gen4+2+(gen1-SolMargin)
			barcode_sol = SolMargin+Gen4+3
		Else
			opr_sol = SolMargin+Gen4+2
			barcode_sol = SolMargin+Gen4+3+(gen1-SolMargin)
		End if
		sure= M10T.R_MIKTAR0(ii)
		sure = Doc.NumFormat(sure,3,2)
		sbirim = M10T.KAYNAK_BIRIM(ii)
		Tezgah = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(ii)
		Operasyon = M10T.R_OPERASYON(ii)
		TezgahAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",Tezgah,"")
		OperasyonAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01","AD",Operasyon,"")
		jobno = M10T.JOBNO(ii)

'		res = pe1.Write(OperasyonMalzemeBalangic+(satiryuksekligi*(2*(ii-1))),opr_sol,satiryuksekligi*2,Gen5,Trim(TezgahAdi)&" / "&Trim(OperasyonAdi))
		res = pe1.Write(OperasyonMalzemeBalangic+(satiryuksekligi*(2*(ii-1))),opr_sol,satiryuksekligi*2,Gen1-7,Trim(TezgahAdi)&" / "&Trim(OperasyonAdi))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 6)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.Barcode(OperasyonMalzemeBalangic+(satiryuksekligi*(2*(ii-1))),barcode_sol,satiryuksekligi*2-1,Gen1-solmargin-5,"CODE128","%JB%"&trim(jobno),"")
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	Next

	Set M10T2 = Doc.Select2("sDRFdsds","MMPS10T","R_KAYNAKTYPE = 'H'","KOD",-1)

	for k = 1 to M10T2.GetRecCount()

		Skod = M10T2.R_KAYNAKKODU(k)
		SkodAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",Skod,"")
		SBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",Skod,"")
		Miktar = M10T2.R_MIKTAR0(k)

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(75+10+1+satiryuksekligi*2+(satiryuksekligi*(k-1)),SolMargin+Gen4*2+Gen5+2,satiryuksekligi,Gen6,Skod)
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,Gen7,SkodAdi)
	res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,Gen8-10,Miktar)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,6," "&Trim(SBirim))

	Next

End Sub

Sub PrintForm()

	Gen2 = 35

	res = pe1.nopen()

Sub Satirlar()
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+28.5,SolMargin+5,15,Gen2,"ÝÞLEM")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,15,Gen2,"MAKÝNA ADI")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,pe1.GetRight,15,Gen2,"OPERATOR ADI")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop-2.5,pe1.GetRight,15,Gen1,"VERILEN SURE"& VBCRLF &"(Dakika)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop+2.5,pe1.GetRight,15,Gen1,"BAÞLAMA TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop-2.5,pe1.GetRight,15,Gen1,"AYAR SÜRESÝ"&VBCRLF&"(Dakika)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop+2.5,pe1.GetRight,15,Gen1,"BAÞLAMA SAATÝ")

End Sub

Sub PrintForm()
	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Set S60E = Doc.Select1("nfdsjhfs","MMPS10E","EVRAKNO = '"& MMPS10E.EVRAKNO &"'","",-1)
	if S60E.GetRecCount() < 1 then
		Exit Sub
		msgbox "Evrak Kaydedilemedi!..."
	End if

	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	
	ustmargin = 10
	solmargin = 8
	
	Gen1 = 50
	Gen2 = 40
	Gen3 = 36
	
	satiryuksekligi = 6
	
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Ýþ Emri", SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")

	Call Baslik()
	Call Satirlar()
	Call OperasyonMalzeme()
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	
End Sub

	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+6,SolMargin+Gen1*2, 0, 281-Gen1*2)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+9,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	
	res = pe1.Write(46,SolMargin, 6,Gen1,"ÜRÜN ADI")

Sub Satirlar()
	
	Gen4 = 10
	Gen5 = 90
	Gen6 = 55
	Gen7 = 75
	Gen8 = 40
	
	res = pe1.Write(75+3,SolMargin, 10,Gen4+Gen5,"OPERASYON SIRALARI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 10,Gen4+Gen6+Gen7+Gen8,"ÜRÜN LÝSTESÝ")
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetBottom-3,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,SolMargin+Gen4, satiryuksekligi*18, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop-10,Pe1.GetRight+Gen5, satiryuksekligi*18+10, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+10+satiryuksekligi,Pe1.GetRight+Gen4, satiryuksekligi*17, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen6, satiryuksekligi*17, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen7, satiryuksekligi*17, 0)
	
	
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop-satiryuksekligi*2,SolMargin, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,i)
	
	for i = 1 to 17
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom,SolMargin, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,i)
		res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetBottom,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	Next
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,SolMargin, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,i)
	
	
	res = pe1.Write(75+10+1,SolMargin+Gen4+Gen5,satiryuksekligi,Gen4+Gen6+Gen7+Gen8,"ALT MALZEME GRUBU")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom,Pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,"S.NO")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi,Gen6,"KODU")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi,Gen7,"ADI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi,Gen8,"ADET(MIKTAR)")
	
	for i = 1 to 16
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom,SolMargin+Gen4+Gen5, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,i)
	Next
	
End Sub

Sub OperasyonMalzeme()
	
	Set M10T = Doc.Select2("sdsds","MMPS10T","R_KAYNAKTYPE = 'I'","",-1)
	operasyonsayisi = M10T.GetRecCount()
	
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	for ii = 1 to operasyonsayisi
		Tezgah = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(ii)
		Operasyon = M10T.R_OPERASYON(ii)
		TezgahAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",Tezgah,"")
		OperasyonAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01","AD",Operasyon,"")
		
		res = pe1.Write(75+10+1+(satiryuksekligi*(ii-1)),SolMargin+Gen4+2,satiryuksekligi,Gen5,Trim(TezgahAdi)&" / "&Trim(OperasyonAdi))
		
		if ii mod 18 = 0 then Exit For
	Next
	
	Set M10T2 = Doc.Select2("sDRFdsds","MMPS10T","R_KAYNAKTYPE = 'H'","KOD",-1)
	hammaddesayisi = M10T2.GetRecCount()
	
	for k = 1 to hammaddesayisi
		
		Skod = M10T2.R_KAYNAKKODU(k)
		SkodAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",Skod,"")
		SBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",Skod,"")
		Miktar = M10T2.R_MIKTAR0(k)
		
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(75+10+1+satiryuksekligi*2+(satiryuksekligi*(k-1)),SolMargin+Gen4*2+Gen5+2,satiryuksekligi,Gen6,Skod)
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,Gen7,SkodAdi)
		res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,Gen8-10,Miktar)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,6," "&Trim(SBirim))
		
		if k mod 16 = 0 then Exit For
	Next
	
	if ii <> operasyonsayisi or k <> hammaddesayisi then
'	if operasyonsayisi > 18 or hammaddesayisi > 16 then
		Call Eksayfa()
	End if
End Sub

Sub Eksayfa()

		pe1.PageBreak()
		
		Call Baslik()
		Call Satirlar()

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		kii = 1
		for iii = ii+1 to operasyonsayisi
			Tezgah = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(iii)
			Operasyon = M10T.R_OPERASYON(iii)
			TezgahAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",Tezgah,"")
			OperasyonAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01","AD",Operasyon,"")
			
			res = pe1.Write(75+10+1+(satiryuksekligi*(kii-1)),SolMargin+Gen4+2,satiryuksekligi,Gen5,Trim(TezgahAdi)&" / "&Trim(OperasyonAdi))
			kii = kii + 1
			if iii mod 18 = 0 then Exit For
		next
			ii = iii

			ikk = 1
		for kk = k+1 to hammaddesayisi
			Skod = M10T2.R_KAYNAKKODU(kk)
			SkodAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",Skod,"")
			SBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",Skod,"")
			Miktar = M10T2.R_MIKTAR0(kk)
			
			res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.Write(75+10+1+satiryuksekligi*2+(satiryuksekligi*(ikk-1)),SolMargin+Gen4*2+Gen5+2,satiryuksekligi,Gen6,Skod)
			res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,Gen7,SkodAdi)
			res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,Gen8-10,Miktar)
			res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,6," "&Trim(SBirim))
			ikk = ikk + 1
			if kk mod 16 = 0 then Exit For
		next
			k = kk

	if iii <= operasyonsayisi or kk <= hammaddesayisi then 
			Call Eksayfa()
		End if
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SENER_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK60-7
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK60I
Açýklama : Sevk Ýrsaliyesi - Aðýrlýklý- Oki
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, sayfaSatirSayisi,S60T, ST60
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirYuksekligi, SagMargin, solmargin, ustmargin, chkodu
Public Grid()
Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1

	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Set S60E = Doc.Select1("nfdsjhfs","STOK60E","EVRAKNO = '"& STOK60E.EVRAKNO &"'","",-1)
	if S60E.GetRecCount() < 1 then Exit Sub

	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 285.5
	sayfaEni = 208.5
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage_P(66)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Irsaliye", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	ustmargin = satiryuksekligi*4
	satirBaslangic = ustmargin + 19 * satirYuksekligi
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 21
	solmargin = 16
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 10

	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK60T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set S60 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	S60T.CopyTable(S60)
	S60T.SafePack
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRowCount()
		CHSipNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", S60T.SIPNO(i), "")
		if trim(CHSipNo) = "" Then CHSipNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40T", "CHSIPANO", S60T.SIPARTNO(i), "")
		S60T.SEVKPAKETLOTNO(i) = CHSipNo
	Next
	S60T.GroupBy("KOD+SEVKPAKETLOTNO")

	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If

	satirSayisi = S60T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Franklin Gothic Medium", 10)
  res = pe1.SetBold(2)
	chkodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU(1)
	chadi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	if trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1) = "" then
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_3(1)
	else
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	end if
	vergidairesi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGIDAIRESI(1)
	vergino = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGINO(1)
	tarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH(1))
	if trim(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH) = "" then
		sevktarih = Tarih
	else
		sevktarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH)
	end if

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 30, satiryuksekligi, 80, trim(chadi))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 13 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 30, satiryuksekligi, 80, adres1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, 80, adres2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, 80, adres3)

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 3 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 165, 5,25, tarih)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 25, sevktarih)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 25, vergidairesi)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 25, vergino)


End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(S60T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If siraNo > sayfaSatirSayisi Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 0
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		End If		

		siraNo = siraNo + 1
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi
		res = pe1.selectfont("Franklin Gothic Medium", 10)
		res = pe1.setbold(2)
		Sol = solmargin+1
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
			DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
			stokkodu = S60T.KOD(satirno)						
			MStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
			MStokAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(stokkodu,chkodu)
			SipNo = S60T.SIPNO(satirno)
			ArtNo = S60T.SIPARTNO(satirno)
			'MSipNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", S60T.SIPNO(satirno), "")
			MSipNo = S60T.SEVKPAKETLOTNO(satirNo)
			Select Case trim(degiskenAdi)
				case "_MKOD"
					degisken = MStokKodu
				case "_MSIPNO"
					degisken = MSipNo
				case else
					Degisken = S60T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
			end select
			If Grid(i,0) = "_SIRANO" Then
				degisken = 	sirano
			ElseIf Grid(i,0) = "_CHSIP" Then
				degisken = 	trim(MSipNo)
			End if

			If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
				res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
				Degisken = Doc.numformat(degisken, grid(i,1), grid(i,6))
			ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
				Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
			End If
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			j = j + 1
		Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	not1 = STOK60E.NOTES_1(1)
	not2 = STOK60E.NOTES_2(1)
	not3 = STOK60E.NOTES_3(1)
	not4 = STOK60E.NOTES_4(1)

	Sofor = STOK60E.BOX1_2
	PlakaNo = STOK60E.BOX1_3

	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+10, satiryuksekligi, 150, not1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+10, satiryuksekligi, 150, not2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+10, satiryuksekligi, 150, not3)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+10, satiryuksekligi, 150, not4)

'	res = pe1.write(240, solmargin + 125, 5, 65, Sofor)
'	res = pe1.write(244, solmargin + 125, 5, 65, PlakaNo)
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRANO"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "_CHSIP"
	Grid(4,0) = ""
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(6,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 10
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 50
	Grid(3,1) = 37
	Grid(4,1) = 20
	Grid(5,1) = 20
	Grid(6,1) = 20
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK60T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SENER_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK63-1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK63
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, sayfaSatirSayisi,S60T, ST60
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirYuksekligi, SagMargin, solmargin, ustmargin, chkodu
Public Grid()
Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	
	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX" Then Doc.Save_Voucher

	Set S60E = Doc.Select1("12AFR","STOK60E","EVRAKNO = '"& STOK60E.EVRAKNO &"'","",-1)
	if S60E.GetRecCount() < 1 then Exit Sub
	siraNo = 0
	
	sayfaBoyu = 285.5
	sayfaEni = 208.5
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage_P(66)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Irsaliye", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	ustmargin = satiryuksekligi*4
	satirBaslangic = ustmargin + 19 * satirYuksekligi
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 21
	solmargin = 16
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK63T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set S60 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK63T")
	S60T.CopyTable(S60)
	S60T.SafePack
	S60T.GroupBy("KOD")
	
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	satirSayisi = S60T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Franklin Gothic Medium", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(2)
	chkodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU(1)
	chadi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	if trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1) = "" then
		adres1 =  STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_1(1)
		adres2 =  STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_2(1)
		adres3 =  STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_3(1)
	else
		adres1 =  STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
		adres2 =  STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
		adres3 =  STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	end if
	vergidairesi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGIDAIRESI(1)
	vergino = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGINO(1)
	tarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH(1))
	if trim(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH) = "" then
		sevktarih = Tarih
	else
		sevktarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH)
	end if
	
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 30, satiryuksekligi, 80, trim(chadi))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 13 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 30, satiryuksekligi, 80, adres1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, 80, adres2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, 80, adres3)
	
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 3 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 165, 5,25, tarih)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 25, sevktarih)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 25, vergidairesi)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 25, vergino)
	
	
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	Dim satirpozr
	If trim(S60T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Degisken = ""
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If siraNo > sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 0
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	End If
	
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirpozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi
	res = pe1.selectfont("Franklin Gothic Medium", 10)
	res = pe1.setbold(2)
	Sol = solmargin+1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		stokkodu = S60T.KOD(satirno)
		MStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
		MStokAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(stokkodu,chkodu)
		SipNo = S60T.SIPNO(satirno)
		ArtNo = S60T.SIPARTNO(satirno)
		MSipNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", sipno, "")
		Select Case trim(degiskenAdi)
			case "_MKOD"
				degisken = MStokKodu &" / "& agirlik &" "& trim(brm)
				'    case "_KG"
				'     brm_katsayi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","CONVUNITS_1",stokkodu,"")
				'     brm = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","UNITS_1",stokkodu,"")
				'     agirlik = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirno) * brm_katsayi
				'     if agirlik > 0 then
				'      degisken = agirlik &" "& trim(brm)
				'     End if
			case "_MSIPNO"
				degisken = MSipNo
			case else
				Degisken = S60T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		end select
		If Grid(i,0) = "_SIRANO" Then
			degisken =  sirano
		ElseIf Grid(i,0) = "_MIKTAR_KG" Then
			miktar = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirno)
			admik = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK00", "CONVUNITS_COEF_1", stokkodu, "")
			kgmik = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK00", "CONVUNITS_1", stokkodu, "")
			kgmiktari = doc.div((miktar * kgmik),admik)
			degisken =  Doc.Numformat(kgmiktari,12,2)
			
		ElseIf Grid(i,0) = "_KG_SF_UNIT" Then
			kgbirim = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK00", "UNITS_1", stokkodu, "")
			degisken = kgbirim
			
			
		End if
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.numformat(degisken, grid(i,1), grid(i,6))
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		j = j + 1
	Next
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	not1 = STOK60E.NOTES_1(1)
	not2 = STOK60E.NOTES_2(1)
	not3 = STOK60E.NOTES_3(1)
	not4 = STOK60E.NOTES_4(1)
	
	Sofor = STOK60E.BOX1_2
	PlakaNo = STOK60E.BOX1_3
	
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+10, satiryuksekligi, 150, not1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+10, satiryuksekligi, 150, not2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+10, satiryuksekligi, 150, not3)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+10, satiryuksekligi, 150, not4)
	
	' res = pe1.write(240, solmargin + 125, 5, 65, Sofor)
	' res = pe1.write(244, solmargin + 125, 5, 65, PlakaNo)
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(8,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRANO"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SEVKPAKETLOTNO"
	Grid(4,0) = "_MIKTAR_KG"
	Grid(5,0) = "_KG_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(7,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 10
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 48
	Grid(3,1) = 27
	Grid(4,1) = 20
	Grid(5,1) = 19
	Grid(6,1) = 16
	Grid(7,1) = 19
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK63T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10 
	SolMargin = 12
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 60
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 10 * satirYuksekligi

	ReDim xCizgi(0)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satinalma Siparisi",  SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
'*******************
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 20)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 3,SolMargin,8,SagMargin - SolMargin,"SATINALMA SÝPARÝÞ FORMU")
		logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
		res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin - 3,SolMargin+5,18,60,logo,114)
		imza = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!IMZA"))
		res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin +155,SolMargin+155,30,75,imza,0)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		SiparisNo = STOK46E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK46E.TARIH(1))
		CHKodu = STOK46E.CH_KODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi = trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2)
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		Faks = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1)
		
		
		SiparisiAlan = STOK46E.SIPIALAN(1)
		SiparisiVeren = STOK46E.SIPIVEREN(1)

		bBasla = UstMargin+17
		SatirAra = 6
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"TEDARÝKÇÝ FÝRMA")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 36, 6,2,": ")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 38, 6,143,CHAdi)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"ADRESÝ")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 36,6,2,": ")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 38,6,85,Adres1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 38, 6,85,Adres2)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 38, 6,85,Adres3)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"FAX NO")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 36, 6,2,": ")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 38, 6,85,Faks)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 182, 6,15,"TARÝH")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 212, 6,50,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 182, 6,26,"SÝPARÝÞ NO")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 212, 6,50,": " & trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 182, 6,26,"ÖDEME ÞEKLÝ")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 212,6,50,": " & trim(STOK46E.ODEMEGUNU(1)) & " gün")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 182, 6,30,"NAKLÝYE ÞEKLÝ")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 212,6,50,": " & trim(STOK46E.NAKLIYECIFIRMA(1)))


' Sütun baþlýklarý
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
'			If i = 3 Or i = 5 Or i = 7 Then
			If i = 4 Or i = 6 Or i = 8 Then
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1)
			Else
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			End If
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 12, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
'					satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 1
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				If satirNo = 1 Then
					xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 1
					ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
				End If
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

	Next

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "NOTE0"
	Grid(3,0) = "RTESTARIH"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(7,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(8,0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	Grid(9,0) = "PRICEUNIT"

'	Grid(0,1) = 51
'	Grid(1,1) = 85
'	Grid(2,1) = 85
'	Grid(3,1) = 20
'	Grid(4,1) = 16
'	Grid(5,1) = 13
'	Grid(6,1) = 30
'	Grid(7,1) = 14
'	Grid(8,1) = 32
'	Grid(9,1) = 14

	Grid(0,1) = 39
	Grid(1,1) = 55
	Grid(2,1) = 30
	Grid(3,1) = 20
	Grid(4,1) = 16
	Grid(5,1) = 13
	Grid(6,1) = 30
	Grid(7,1) = 14
	Grid(8,1) = 32
	Grid(9,1) = 14

	Grid(0,2) = "MALZEME KODU"
	Grid(1,2) = "MALZEME ADI"
	Grid(2,2) = "NOT"
	Grid(3,2) = "TESLÝM TARÝHÝ"
	Grid(4,2) = "MÝKTAR ve BÝRÝM"
	Grid(5,2) = ""
	Grid(6,2) = "FÝYAT"
	Grid(7,2) = ""
	Grid(8,2) = "TUTAR"
	Grid(9,2) = ""
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "D"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"
	Grid(9,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
'			ondalik = DecimalHesapla(Degisken)	
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
			If i = 7 Then
				ToplamTutar = STOK46E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
				dToplamTutar = Doc.SigDig(ToplamTutar)
				IskontoToplami=STOK46E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
				dIskontoToplami = Doc.SigDig(IskontoToplami)
				NetTutar = STOK46E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
				dNetTutar = Doc.SigDig(NetTutar)
				KDVTutari = STOK46E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
				dKDVTutari = Doc.SigDig(KDVTutari)
				KDVDahilTutar = STOK46E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
				dKDVDahilTutar = Doc.SigDig(KDVDahilTutar)

				Ondalik1 = Ondalik 
				If dIskontoToplami > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dIskontoToplami
				ElseIf dNetTutar > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dNetTutar
				ElseIf dKDVTutari > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dKDVTutari
				ElseIf dKDVDahilTutar > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dKDVDahilTutar
				End If

				If Ondalik1 > 2 Then Ondalik1 = 2
				grid(i,6) = Ondalik1
			End If
		End If
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SENER_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : MPS-1/1
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Þener Otomotiv Ýþ Emri Formu
Kod : 
Public ustmargin, solmargin,Gen1,Gen2,Gen3,pe1,satiryuksekligi,Gen4,Gen5,Gen6,Gen7,Gen8,operasyonsayisi,hammaddesayisi
Public M10T, M10T2,oprsatir2,hmdsatir2

Sub PrintForm()

	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Set S60E = Doc.Select1("nfdsjhfs","MMPS10E","EVRAKNO = '"& MMPS10E.EVRAKNO &"'","",-1)
	if S60E.GetRecCount() < 1 then
		Exit Sub
		msgbox "Evrak Kaydedilemedi!..."
	End if

	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	
	ustmargin = 10
	solmargin = 8

	oprsatir2 = 1
	hmdsatir2 = 1
	
	Gen1 = 50
	Gen2 = 40
	Gen3 = 36
	
	satiryuksekligi = 6
	
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Ýþ Emri", SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	
	
	Call Baslik()
	Call Satirlar()
	
	Set M10T = Doc.Select2("sdsds","MMPS10T","R_KAYNAKTYPE = 'I'","",-1)
	operasyonsayisi = M10T.GetRecCount()
	oprsayfasayisi = Doc.Ceil(operasyonsayisi/18)

	Set M10T2 = Doc.Select2("sDRFdsds","MMPS10T","R_KAYNAKTYPE = 'H'","KOD",-1)
	hammaddesayisi = M10T2.GetRecCount()
	hmdsayfasayisi = Doc.Ceil(hammaddesayisi/16)

	if hmdsayfasayisi > oprsayfasayisi then
		for zz= 1 to hmdsayfasayisi
			Call Baslik()
			Call Satirlar()
			Call Operasyon()
			Call Malzeme()
			if zz <> hmdsayfasayisi then pe1.PageBreak()
		Next
	Else
		for zz= 1 to oprsayfasayisi
			Call Baslik()
			Call Satirlar()
			Call Operasyon()
			Call Malzeme()
			if zz <> oprsayfasayisi then pe1.PageBreak()
		Next
	End if

	res = pe1.Show()
	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	
End Sub


Sub Baslik()
	
	res = pe1.nopen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin+1, solMargin+1, 18, Gen2+3, logo, 96)
	
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 24)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+4,SolMargin+5+Gen2, 16,275-5-Gen2,"Ý Þ   E M R Ý   T A K Ý P   F O R M U")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	
	res = pe1.box(UstMargin,SolMargin, 183, 281)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+20,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin,SolMargin+Gen2+5, 20, 0)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	
	' res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom+5,SolMargin, 6,Gen1*2,"SÝPARÝÞ VERÝLERÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(35,SolMargin, 6,Gen1*2,"SÝPARÝÞ VERÝLERÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop-4,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,"SÝPARÝÞ MÝKTARI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,"PARTÝ MÝKTARI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,"SIRA NO")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,"SÝPARÝÞ TÜRÜ")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,"SÝPARÝÞ NO")
	
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select2("sfghe3dsds","MMPS10TM","","",1)
	
	Artno = M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(1)
	
	Set S40T = Doc.Select1("sf213ds","STOK40T","ARTNO = '"& Artno &"'","",1)
	if S40T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		sipmik = S40T.SF_MIKTAR(1)
		sipno = M10TM.MUSTERISIPINO(1)
	End if

	parmik = "Parti Mik"
	sirano = "sýra no"
	siptur = "Sip tür"
	
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+7,SolMargin+Gen1*2, 6,Gen3,Doc.NumFormat(sipmik,10,0))
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,parmik)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,sirano)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,siptur)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen3,sipno)
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop-1-7,SolMargin+Gen1*2, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen3, 15, 0)
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+6,SolMargin+Gen1*2, 0, 281-Gen1*2)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+9,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	
	res = pe1.Write(46,SolMargin, 6,Gen1,"ÜRÜN ADI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1,"GRUP ADI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1*2,"PARÇA ADI")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8.5)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2,"SÝPARÝÞÝN VER. TARÝH")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2,"MUHTEMEL SÝP. TES. TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	
	urun = "ürün"
	grup = "grup"
	parca = "parca"
	pkodu = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	parca = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",pkodu,"")
	siptar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","TARIH",sipno ,"")
	testar = MMPS10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH
	
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+7,SolMargin, 6,Gen1,urun)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1,grup)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1*2,trim(parca))
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2,Doc.StrToNorDate(siptar))
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2,Doc.StrToNorDate(testar))
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop-1-7,SolMargin+Gen1, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen1, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen1*2, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen2, 15, 0)
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+6,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+9,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom+1,SolMargin, 6,Gen1*2,"PARÇA KODU")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1*2,"RESÝM NO")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2*2,"ÝÞ EMRÝ NO")
	
'	pkodu = "pkodu"
	resimno = "resimno"
	isemrino = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
	
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+7,SolMargin, 6,Gen1*2,trim(pkodu))
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen1*2,resimno)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 6,Gen2*2,isemrino)
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop-1-7,SolMargin+Gen1*2, 15, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen1*2, 15, 0)
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+6,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+9,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	
End Sub

Sub Satirlar()
	
	Gen4 = 10
	Gen5 = 90
	Gen6 = 55
	Gen7 = 75
	Gen8 = 40
	
	res = pe1.Write(75+3,SolMargin, 10,Gen4+Gen5,"OPERASYON SIRALARI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, 10,Gen4+Gen6+Gen7+Gen8,"ÜRÜN LÝSTESÝ")
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetBottom-3,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,SolMargin+Gen4, satiryuksekligi*18, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop-10,Pe1.GetRight+Gen5, satiryuksekligi*18+10, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop+10+satiryuksekligi,Pe1.GetRight+Gen4, satiryuksekligi*17, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen6, satiryuksekligi*17, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight+Gen7, satiryuksekligi*17, 0)
	
	
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop-satiryuksekligi*2,SolMargin, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,i)
	
	for i = 1 to 17
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom,SolMargin, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,i)
		res = pe1.Line(Pe1.GetBottom,SolMargin, 0, 281)
	Next
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,SolMargin, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,i)
	
	res = pe1.Write(75+10+1,SolMargin+Gen4+Gen5,satiryuksekligi,Gen4+Gen6+Gen7+Gen8,"ALT MALZEME GRUBU")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom,Pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,"S.NO")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi,Gen6,"KODU")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi,Gen7,"ADI")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi,Gen8,"ADET(MIKTAR)")
	
	for i = 1 to 16
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom,SolMargin+Gen4+Gen5, satiryuksekligi,Gen4,i)
	Next
	
End Sub

Sub Operasyon()
	
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	oprsatir = 0

	for ii = oprsatir2 to operasyonsayisi
		Tezgah = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(ii)
		Operasyonkodu = M10T.R_OPERASYON(ii)
		TezgahAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",Tezgah,"")
		OperasyonAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01","AD",Operasyonkodu,"")
		
'		res = pe1.Write(86+(satiryuksekligi*oprsatir),SolMargin+Gen4+2,satiryuksekligi,Gen5,Trim(TezgahAdi)&" / "&Trim(OperasyonAdi))
		res = pe1.Write(75+10+1+(satiryuksekligi*oprsatir),SolMargin+Gen4+2,satiryuksekligi,Gen5,Trim(TezgahAdi)&" / "&Trim(OperasyonAdi))
		
		oprsatir = oprsatir + 1
		oprsatir2 = oprsatir2 + 1
		if ii mod 18 = 0 then Exit For
	Next
	
End Sub

Sub Malzeme()
	hmdsatir = 0
	for k = hmdsatir2 to hammaddesayisi
		
		Skod = M10T2.R_KAYNAKKODU(k)
		SkodAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",Skod,"")
		SBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",Skod,"")
		Miktar = M10T2.R_MIKTAR0(k)
		
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(86+(satiryuksekligi*2)+(satiryuksekligi*hmdsatir),SolMargin+Gen4*2+Gen5+2,satiryuksekligi,Gen6,Skod)
		res = pe1.Write(75+10+1+satiryuksekligi*2+(satiryuksekligi*hmdsatir),SolMargin+Gen4*2+Gen5+2,satiryuksekligi,Gen6,Skod)
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,Gen7,SkodAdi)
		res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,Gen8-10,Miktar)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop,Pe1.GetRight,satiryuksekligi,6," "&Trim(SBirim))
		
		hmdsatir = hmdsatir + 1
		hmdsatir2 = hmdsatir2 + 1
		if k mod 16 = 0 then Exit For
	Next
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SENER_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : FATURA
Hangi Tablo için : STOK70
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK70I
Açýklama : FATURA
Kod : 
Dim satirAraligi, UstMargin, SolMargin, 	SayfaEni, AltMargin, SayfaBoyu, SagMargin, ara_toplam,S70T
Dim Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Set S60E = Doc.Select1("nfdsjhfs","STOK70E","EVRAKNO = '"& STOK70E.EVRAKNO &"'","",-1)
	if S60E.GetRecCount() < 1 then Exit Sub

	SayfaBoyu = 297
	SayfaEni  = 225
	SolMargin = 14
	satirAraligi = 4.3
	UstMargin = satirAraligi * 10
	ara_toplam = 0

	STOK70T.SafePack()
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"ÞENER - FATURA", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni,"")

	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(6) 
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage_P(66)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetFormLength(11)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)

	evrakno = STOK70E.EVRAKNO
	Set S70T = Doc.Select2("sdasdas","STOK70T","","",-1)
	S70T.SafePack
	For i = 1 to S70T.GetRowCount()
		CHSipNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", S70T.SIPNO(i), "")
		if trim(CHSipNo) = "" Then CHSipNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40T", "CHSIPANO", S70T.S60_SIPARTNO(i), "")
		S70T.SEVKPAKETLOTNO(i) = CHSipNo
	Next
	S70T.GroupBy("KOD+SEVKPAKETLOTNO")

		Call Alanlar(pe1)	
		Call Ust_Bolum(pe1)	
		For i = 1 To S70T.GetRecCount() 
			Call Satir_Yaz(pe1, i) 	
		Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Ust_Bolum(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("courier new",7)

	MusteriCariKodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU
	EvrakNo =  STOK70E.EVRAKNO
	Musteri = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "AD" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
	MusterAdresi1 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "ADRES_1" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
	MusterAdresi2 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "ADRES_2" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
	MusterAdresi3 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "ADRES_3" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
	EvrakTarihi = STOK70E.TARIH
	MusteriVergiDairesi = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "VERGIDAIRESI" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
	MusteriVergiNo = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "VERGINO" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
	Tckimlikno = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "TCNO" , MusteriCariKodu , "")

	Set a_STOK70 = Doc.Select2G("_sadas_sad", "STOK70T", "", "","IRSALIYENO", -1)

'	irsaliye_tarihi = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("STOK60E" , "TARIH" , (S70T.IRSALIYENO(1)), "")

	irsaliye_no = ""
	irsaliye_tarihi = ""

	for i = 1 TO a_STOK70.GetRecCount()
		if i = 1 then 
			irsaliye_no = trim(a_STOK70.IRSALIYENO(i))
			irsaliye_tarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK60E", "TARIH", irsaliye_no, "") )
		else
			irsaliye_no  = trim(irsaliye_no  & " " & a_STOK70.IRSALIYENO(i))
			irsaliye_tarihi = irsaliye_tarihi & " " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK60E", "TARIH", irsaliye_no, "") )
		end if 
	next
	
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, SolMargin +5,satirAraligi, 95 , Trim(Musteri))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi, SolMargin +5,satirAraligi, 95 , Trim(MusterAdresi1))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 2, SolMargin +5, satirAraligi, 65 , Trim(MusterAdresi2))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 3, SolMargin +5, satirAraligi, 65 , Trim(MusterAdresi3))

	sag_konum = 190
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin , SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 18 , Doc.StrToNorDate(EvrakTarihi))

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi, SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 15 , irsaliye_tarihi)	
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 2, SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 15 , irsaliye_no)
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 4, SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 15 , Trim(MusteriVergiDairesi))
	
res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 5,  SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 15 , Trim(MusteriVergiNo) )
IF TRIM(Tckimlikno)<>"" THEN
res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 5,  SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 15 , Trim(Tckimlikno) )
End if

End Sub

Sub Satir_Yaz(pe1, satirno) 
	Dim satir_bilgisi, saga_kayma, Flag_saga_devam_mi 
	if satirno = 1 Then
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 9 , Solmargin , 1 , 1, "") 		
	End if
	if satirno > 1 Then
	 res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,Solmargin,1,1, " ") '// Hizalama iþlemi 
	End if
	Flag_saga_devam_mi = 0
	For i = 0 To Ubound (Grid,2)
		satir_bilgisi = S70T.GetFieldValue(Grid(2, i) ,satirno)
		if Grid(2, i) = "AD" Then
			satir_bilgisi = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", Trim(S70T.KOD(satirno)), "")
		Elseif Grid(2, i) = "_OR_FIYAT" Then
			satir_bilgisi = Doc.Div(S70T.OR_TUTAR(satirno),S70T.SF_MIKTAR(satirno))
		Elseif Grid(2, i) = "_CHSIPNO" Then
			satir_bilgisi = S70T.SEVKPAKETLOTNO(satirNo)
		End if


		if Trim(Grid(2, i)) = "NO" Then  satir_bilgisi = satirno 
			baslangic = Grid(1,i)
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if Grid(3,i) = "N" And 	Flag_saga_devam_mi = 0 Then '// En baþ sütuna rakamsal deðer text olarak basýlacak
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			saga_kayma = -2
			Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1  
		Elseif Grid(3,i) = "N" And Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1 Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			saga_kayma = 0 
			Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1  
		Elseif Grid(3,i) <> "N" And Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1 Then
			saga_kayma = 2
			Flag_saga_devam_mi = 0
		End if
		if Grid(3,i) = "N" Then
			satir_bilgisi = Doc.numformat0(satir_bilgisi, 16,Grid(4,i))			
		Else
			satir_bilgisi = Trim(satir_bilgisi)
		End if
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop  , pe1.GetRight + saga_kayma , satirAraligi , baslangic, satir_bilgisi)
		saga_kayma = 0
	Next

	ara_toplam = ara_toplam + S70T.TL_TUTAR(satirno)
	konumlandirma = 170
	konumlandirma_yazi = 120
'	genislik_yazi = 30
	genislik_yazi = 37
'	genislik_deger = 30
	genislik_deger = 37

	if pe1.GetTop() > UstMargin + satirAraligi * 36 And satirno < S70T.GetRecCount() Then

		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "==============")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma_yazi - 5, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "ARA TOPLAM")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop , SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi,	genislik_deger,Doc.numformat0(ara_toplam,12,2))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Ust_Bolum(pe1)
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 13,SolMargin,1,1, "") '// Hizalama iþlemi
	End if

	if satirno = S70T.GetRecCount() Then


		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "==============")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma_yazi - 5, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "TOPLAM")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop , SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi,	genislik_deger,Doc.numformat0(STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1),12,2))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "==============")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma_yazi - 5, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "KDV % " & Doc.numformaT(STOK70E.DO_KDV_ORANI(1),4,0))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop , SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi,	genislik_deger,Doc.numformat0(STOK70E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM(1),12,2))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "==============")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma_yazi - 5, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "GENEL TOPLAM")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop , SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi,	genislik_deger,Doc.numformat0(STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1),12,2))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "==============")


		'res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		'res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma_yazi - 5, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi,"TEVKÝFAT")
		'res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		'res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop , SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi,	genislik_deger,Doc.numformat0(STOK70E.TL_KESINTI1(1),12,2))

		'res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		'res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma_yazi - 5, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi,"BEYAN EDÝLEN KDV")
		'res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		'res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop , SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi,	genislik_deger,Doc.numformat0(STOK70E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM(1) - STOK70E.TL_KESINTI1(1),12,2))
		'res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "==============")
	'	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	'	res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma_yazi - 5, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "GENEL TOPLAM")
	'	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	'	res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop , SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi,	genislik_deger,Doc.numformat0(STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1),12,2))
	'	res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,SolMargin + konumlandirma, satirAraligi, genislik_yazi, "==============")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi*4, SolMargin + 15, satirAraligi, 100,"YALNIZ : " &Replace(Doc.NumToText(STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)),"%","TL") & "Kr")
		
		End if 
End Sub

Sub Alanlar(pe1)
	ReDim Grid(4,9)

'// Alan Geniþlikleri

	Grid(1,0) = 10
	Grid(1,1) = 5
	Grid(1,2) = 30
	Grid(1,3) = 48
	Grid(1,4) = 20
	Grid(1,5) = 25
	Grid(1,6) = 3
	Grid(1,7) = 15
	Grid(1,8) = 20
	Grid(1,9) = 32

'// Satýr Ýþlemleri

	Grid(2,0) = "NO"
	Grid(2,1) = "_NO"
	Grid(2,2) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,3) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,4) = "_CHSIPNO"
	Grid(2,5) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(2,6) = "_NO"
	Grid(2,7) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(2,8) = "_OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(2,9) = "OR_TUTAR"

'// Hizalama

	Grid(3,0) = "N"
	Grid(3,1) = "T"
	Grid(3,2) = "T"
	Grid(3,3) = "T"
	Grid(3,4) = "T"
	Grid(3,5) = "N"
	Grid(3,6) = "T"
	Grid(3,7) = "T"
	Grid(3,8) = "N"
	Grid(3,9) = "N"

'// Ondalýk

	Grid(4,0) = 0
	Grid(4,1) = 0
	Grid(4,2) = 0
	Grid(4,3) = 0
	Grid(4,4) = 0
	Grid(4,5) = 2
	Grid(4,6) = 0
	Grid(4,7) = 0
	Grid(4,8) = 2
	Grid(4,9) = 2

End Sub
